# Original Eagle, Pig and Boar

The recovered Eagle art keeps eight original motions and adds linked flight transitions, an airborne fall and the native static corpse. The two isolated profiles passed 168 checks across Eagle, Phoenix and Enraged Eagle. See [Original Eagle](EAGLE_ORIGINAL.md).

Pig and Boar retain their separate Third Dawn coats and stock identities. Local rotations preserve the Boar's limb scales and prepare the hooves for nearby treads. Both modes passed 9,000 moving poses and 148 isolated native checks combined. See [Pig and Boar terrain](PORCINE_TERRAIN.md).


# Goats, Grizzly and Grey Wolf on steps

The source rigs now share four-foot terrain support, a continuous collapse and a static corpse with the native body and hue. Both modes passed 22,500 moving poses, 352 decoded death transfers and 416 isolated server checks. See [Goat, Grizzly and Grey Wolf terrain](QUADRUPED_TERRAIN.md) for the Enhanced gestures, exact source members and remaining ledge motion.


# Ratman, Giant Spider, Cow and Skeleton footing

The five Ratman body routes keep their existing art and actions, with corrected walking loops, step clearance and a continuous fall to the static corpse. Both profiles passed 6,360 moving poses combined, 256 corpse transitions and 218 isolated native checks. See [Ratman terrain](RATMAN_TERRAIN.md).

The Giant Spider uses its original eight-legged rig on slopes and steps. The server's movement now drives its position without adding the source clip's forward travel a second time. Its original falls continue into matching static corpses. See [Giant Spider terrain](GIANT_SPIDER_TERRAIN.md).

Both Cow coats use the shared hoof support, keep the stock living tip and recover into standing or movement. Two isolated clients used actual pitchers and confirmed the owner's fill and the foreign player's refusal. See [Cow terrain](COW_TERRAIN.md).

Both Skeleton bodies keep their original rigs and body 56 keeps its axe. The Enhanced assembly fits the loose bones to the terrain and completes the join before a native death during the scatter. The isolated server trials also verified both Spellbinder rolls and Szandor. See [Skeleton terrain](SKELETON_TERRAIN.md).

# Ratman Archer

The stock Ratman Archer uses its original rig, recovered bow, separate item dye, and a release pose driven by the native arrow packet. Both client profiles passed 45 normal World checks and 31 isolated server checks. The stock corpse keeps its body and hue after the server removes the worn bow. See [Ratman Archer](RATMAN_ARCHER.md) for the source and test details.


# Bull footing and corpse handoff

Both Bull bodies passed 13,000 terrain samples, 312 corpse handoffs and 80 native checks covering taming, movement, combat, hues and corpses. See [Bull terrain](BULL_TERRAIN.md).


# Cat terrain and corpse continuity

Cat, Hell Cat and Diseased Cat use their existing licensed body with bounded paw bends and exact two-tread clearance. The client passed 6,760 sampled poses, 156 fall transfers and 556 clean-distribution checks. The isolated server passed 172 checks, including Hell Cat's autonomous fire breath and matching native corpse hues. See [Cat terrain](CAT_TERRAIN.md) for the measured high-step limits.


# Panther and talisman Panther on uneven ground

Body 214 keeps its original Third Dawn rig, atlas and Classic action routes. The client passed 2,600 terrain poses, 72 corpse handoffs and both World profiles. Two isolated clients passed 58 native checks, including wild taming, stock talisman activation and both native static corpses. See [Panther terrain](PANTHER_TERRAIN.md) for the exact source audit and tests.


# Dog terrain and native coat hues

The original hound now has checked step support and collapse continuity. Two isolated clients passed 58 checks for server coat hues, stock taming and the visible corpse. See [Dog terrain](DOG_TERRAIN.md) for coverage and reproduction.


# Cougar terrain and collapse

The original Cougar now clears the sampled steps and slopes. Native death carries over the displayed pose, and late dye keeps the settled geometry. Two isolated clients passed 58 checks, including stock taming. See [Cougar terrain](COUGAR_TERRAIN.md) for the coverage and reproduction steps.


# Black and Brown Bear terrain

Both original bears now clear slopes and steps with four-paw support. The full-skin sweeps covered 5,200 Classic and 8,840 Enhanced poses. Four native identities passed 105 isolated server checks, including two ordinary taming cases and Grobu's fur loot. See [Bear terrain](BEAR_TERRAIN.md) for the coverage and reproduction steps.


# Common Troll terrain and corpse handoff

The original axe, unarmed and Frost Troll bodies clear the sampled slopes and 22 cm step. The optional action renderer uses the correct visible weapon and freezes that state with the server body and hue. Two isolated clients passed 127 checks across six native constructor cases. See [Troll terrain](TROLL_TERRAIN.md) for coverage and build steps.


# Armed Lizardman living terrain

The original spear and mace bodies now correct their living feet, tail and weapon on slopes and low steps. The client passed 3,672 planar poses and 1,728 step poses, with less than 10 mm of whole-model lift on the tested step. A running creature can leave an idle fidget. The isolated server passed 74 native checks for both weapon forms and player sexes, including rummage refusal and permitted loot transfer. See [reproduction and evidence](ARMED_LIZARDMAN_TERRAIN.md).


# Enhanced Timber Wolf and Injured Wolf

Body 225 keeps its original Third Dawn rig, coat and Classic clips. Six optional motions use the Grey Wolf reference frames and matched 32-joint rest rig. The Enhanced archive has no direct Timber Wolf sequence. The client passed 4,860 terrain poses and 162 fall transfers. The isolated server passed 125 checks across the Timber Wolf and Injured Wolf with both player sexes, including taming, pack behavior and Haochi's third trial. See [build and evidence](TIMBER_WOLF_ENHANCED.md).


# Ordinary Rat Enhanced motions

The small licensed Rat now has seven fitted gestures linked to body 215 by the later Third Dawn table. The fitted scene keeps the existing thirteen animation clips and uses the small rig's two tail joints and four ground contacts. The corrected ear placement follows the existing head weights. The normal client passed 11 Classic and 106 Enhanced checks, 1,890 skinned terrain poses and 63 fall transfers. Two isolated clients passed 119 native checks for Rat and Sewer Rat. See the [Ordinary Rat build and evidence](ORDINARY_RAT_ENHANCED.md).


# Enhanced Sea, Giant and Silver Serpents

The optional Enhanced profiles retain the original Third Dawn rigs and separate atlases. Sea and Deep Sea Serpents share fourteen source groups. Giant Serpent, Death Adder and serpent-shaped Plague Spawn use fifteen source groups. Silver Serpent receives nine fits on its own rig from the donor named by the Third Dawn table. The native clients passed 75 Sea, 129 Giant and 47 Silver presentation checks. An additional Silver trial passed 56 stock charm and poison checks. See the [Sea](SEA_SERPENT_ENHANCED.md), [Giant](GIANT_SERPENT_ENHANCED.md) and [Silver](SILVER_SERPENT_ENHANCED.md) evidence and build steps.


# Original Silver, faction, Ethereal and Pack Horses

Seven original Third Dawn forms now have tested optional Enhanced presentation. Silver and Holy Steed share body 117. The four faction horses use their own original coats, the Ethereal Horse uses historical artwork through its native worn item, and Pack Horse keeps its original cargo on the living rig and static corpse. Nineteen fitted clips and six retained gaits cover the 25 indexed groups for True Britannians Warhorse and Pack Horse. Both human rigs and the stock mounting, faction, ethics and pack routes passed 561 native checks across 38 cases. The Holy Steed breath passed 23 more native checks. See [Original war horses](ORIGINAL_WAR_HORSES.md) for the build and evidence.

# Original skeletal mounts

The optional Enhanced body 793 retains the original 34-joint skeletal horse for Skeletal Mount, Hell Steed, Grizzled Mare and Red Death. Nine fits and four retained motions cover thirteen groups from 670 verified Enhanced reference frames. Isolated native tests passed 175 riding and corpse checks, including Red Death’s original bridle loot, and 55 Chaos Breath checks. Both riders passed 108 fitted poses, and the client preserved both fitted falls, the original Classic fall, and the server’s hues. See [Skeletal mounts](SKELETAL_MOUNTS.md) for local build and verification.


# Original Nightmare, Fire Steed and Unholy Steed

The optional Enhanced profile uses two original Third Dawn models, their separate coats and eight table-linked original clips. The native body/item pairs remain authoritative. The authored fire-breath motion uses the stock action selector and the stock projectile starts at the animated muzzle. The final isolated tests passed 195 riding and ethics checks, 71 stock fire-breath checks, 432 rider poses and 36 terrain-to-corpse transfers. See [Dark horses](DARK_HORSES.md) for build steps and scope.


# Enhanced Desert and Forest Ostards

The optional Enhanced profile covers Desert Ostard body 210 and Forest Ostard body 219 with their original Third Dawn rigs and separate coats. Three existing source movement clips and fifteen fitted clips cover all 18 official groups per form, including combat, peaceful and seated movement, a leaping kick and two falls. Both falls transfer to grounded static corpses. The Classic Desert routes still select their original Classic actions.

The official Enhanced sequence hashes are `0xc52bb7353f8a8faa` and `0x996796c22ae4d572`. The registered `AnimationFrame3.uop` members supplied 835 frame pairs in five directions. The silhouettes overlap by at least 99.767% at the original registrations, so both forms share the fitted motion. Their early and later Third Dawn models retain the same topology and skin weights. The ModernUO body and worn item determine the named Forest or Desert art.

Two isolated ModernUO clients passed 87 checks for both rider sexes, including stock mounting, walk, run, dismount, full and partial dye and native static corpses. The fixture sets controlled ownership and sends an explicit recoil selector. The Forest constructor's partial hue passes through both clients. Classic and Enhanced replays passed 26 and 188 checks, including both corpse packet orders and late dye. The terrain suite measured 5,346 poses and 54 fall transfers. The largest sampled joint displacement during a handoff was 2.123 mm. The final resting pelvis heights stayed under 0.299 m. Rider checks covered 288 poses with measured bent elbows, and 24 Forward+ captures show both mounts.

After preparing the local Third Dawn Ostard scenes, run Godot's `scripts/bake-enhanced-source-ostards.gd`, then run `python -B scripts/install-enhanced-source-ostards.py`. The client validates the six local scenes before selecting the profile. The original archives, baked art and screenshots stay in local storage.


# Original ki-rin and unicorn mounts

Living ki-rin body 132 and unicorn body 122 now use their own later Third Dawn models, colour atlases and rigs in the optional Enhanced profile. The ki-rin retains its 30-joint rig and alpha-cutout mane. The living unicorn has a 38-joint rig and an original seven-vertex horn on the head. The unicorn body palette was verified against the table-linked dappled horse weights. Six of the nine original horn faces have coincident points and stay invisible under all rigid poses. The three visible faces use normals reconstructed from their winding.

The official Enhanced files yielded all 35 named groups for the two living mounts, with 910 registered ki-rin frames and 675 unicorn frames. Twenty-nine fitted clips and six retained original movement clips cover those groups. The client selects the original combat, peaceful and ki-rin mounted gaits, plus body-specific attacks, recoil, casting, alerts and two falls. The ki-rin's original hover is a client animation selection under the flight flag. Pinned ModernUO retains its own movement authority.

Ethereal ki-rin and unicorn use their separate original Third Dawn rigs and atlases when native items `0x3E9C` and `0x3E9B` are worn. The ethereal ki-rin uses the living cutout mask after verification that triangle and texture coordinates register exactly. Its RGB still comes from the original spectral atlas. Both forms preserve the default ethereal hue and full and partial server dyes.

Normal client replay passed 426 checks, including both corpse packet orders, late dye and the two fitted falls. Terrain checks covered 2,091 skinned poses and 36 fall transfers. The largest joint displacement during a fall transfer was 0.68 micrometres. Rider checks passed 1,033 assertions across 180 poses with 18 Forward+ captures. The seat, stirrups, reins and bent elbows were measured on the normal rider rigs. Two native clients passed 129 checks across both living and both ethereal classes for both riders. Pinned ModernUO allowed male riders on the living ki-rin, female riders on the living unicorn, and both on the ethereal forms. The test fixture controls ownership and the recoil selector. Stock mounting, movement, dismount, dye, death and corpses pass through the normal server and client paths.

To build locally, run `python -B scripts/prepare-magical-mounts.py --models <private Third Dawn Models directory> --install`. The recipe reads the extracted `Animals`, `Monsters` and `Maps` folders and writes its four bundled scenes and manifest under `Viewer/local-art/mounts`. Launch with the prepared Enhanced profile. The original game inputs, generated scenes, source-frame exports and screenshots remain private.


# Enhanced llamas and original Pack Llama

The optional Enhanced llama presentation keeps the original 29-joint rig and seven Third Dawn motions for ordinary and rideable body 220. Seven fitted motions use 445 registered Enhanced reference frames from ten members of `AnimationFrame4.uop` and sequence `0x676fc8f05e092c96`. Combat walking lowers the head by 23.2 cm on average with no measured change to the original hoof trajectories. The rear strike, recoil, quiet idle, forward check, paw alert and rear-and-fall have separate motion. Riding keeps the neutral gaits. Classic group 6 retains the original attack for the Energy Vortex llama. The ethereal item `0x3EAB` keeps its separate spectral presentation.

Pack Llama body 292 uses its own original 30-joint asset. The recovered file `llamas_llama_pack_lod2.grn` in `models/animals/client8.cab` has a 120-vertex skinned body and a separate 20-vertex rigid load. The two surface groups share the declared `llamas_llama_v04.tga` atlas from `models/maps/client10.cab`. The body and load use different texture regions. The source body weight buffer matches the ordinary llama byte for byte and starts at offset one in the shared palette. Index zero belongs to the load joint under the spine. The conversion keeps the original load mesh, texture coordinates and seven original gaits and actions. Subdivision rounds the body with a measured displacement cap and preserves the hooves. The modern mesh has 5,828 triangles, including the original 36 load triangles. Classic mode continues to use the established local panniers.

The original and modern Pack Llama passed 42 hardware-rendered views and 350 full-skin samples. The load joint moved about 11 cm during the gait with less than a micrometre of measured change in the sampled rigid edge. The flat source bake touched about 3 mm below its plane at the lowest sample. The client support checks passed 1,404 skin samples over three finite slopes and three headings. The 36 fall handoffs displaced a joint by at most 0.76 micrometres. The normal World checks covered 153 Enhanced and four Classic cases, including actual animation playback, both corpse packet orders, the load surface on the static corpse and the late dye packet.

Two isolated ModernUO clients passed 141 checks across the four constructors with both human rigs. The stock server allowed the rideable and ethereal forms and refused the ordinary and pack forms. Movement, full and partial mount hues, dismount, loose motion, death and static cadavers passed. The test explicitly sets fixture ownership and sends a controlled recoil packet. It exercises the stock client/server paths for mounting, movement, item replication and death.

## Preparing local art

With the ordinary llama already prepared by `scripts/prepare-distinct-mounts.py`, run Godot with `--path Viewer --headless --script res://../scripts/bake-llama-enhanced.gd -- --output-dir=<absolute private directory>`. To include the original load, first run `python -B scripts/recover-pack-llama.py --models <private Third Dawn Models directory> --output-dir <private working directory>`. Pass the resulting absolute `--pack-model=<directory>/pack-llama-modern.gltf` to the bake. Place the generated `llama-enhanced.json`, `llama-enhanced.res` and optional `pack-llama-modern.scn` in `Viewer/local-art/mounts`. Launch with the prepared Enhanced profile using `--enhanced-creature-actions`.

The source recovery script validates each input member against its recorded SHA-256. It retains only local assets. Original meshes, atlases, captures and generated binary scenes are excluded from Git.


# Distinct Enhanced Sea Horse

Sea Horse body 144 and ridden item 0x3EB3 now have a distinct Enhanced presentation. The original Third Dawn sea horse colour atlas was recovered. The inspected later cabinets contained fourteen human rider animation members and two sea horse atlases. Their audited rider files contain animation tracks and no creature mesh. The new rig and mesh were authored from the 765 registered official frames in all seventeen members of `AnimationFrame4.uop` and the sequence `0xed9ffc90ed69c840`. The private material adds scale relief and uses native full and partial hues. Mounted Sea Horses have fitted leather saddles and stirrups.

The seventeen fitted clips use 2,482 vertices, 4,940 triangles and 13 joints, including the mouth, saddle and fin anchors. Forward+ rendered 51 loose views and six ridden views with both human rigs. The focused tests passed 312 checks, including 216 weighted terrain poses over nine fall handoffs. The largest sampled position change at a handoff was 0.12 micrometres. The normal client replay passed three Classic and 24 Enhanced checks. Both Enhanced falls end in static corpses and preserve their position and geometry after a partial dye packet.

The staged normal client and a second isolated ModernUO client passed 47 checks. Both rider sexes used the stock double-click mount and dismount paths, walked and ran mounted and loose, replicated full and partial dyes, and observed a stock death and the static native corpse. The fixture controlled ownership and sent the recoil selector. The stock walking, non-swimming and untamable flags remain authoritative. The current Classic client uses its documented horse-art fallback.

## Preparing the Sea Horse locally

Run `python -B scripts/prepare-seahorse.py --atlas <private sea_horse_sea_horse.tga> --output-dir <private working directory>`. The script requires the recovered original atlas with SHA-256 `67a3fbf5dcd5e5479a67720c0e8d371fbb4f49688a965e8b47c9ef5e0898c68b`. Run Godot with `--path Viewer --headless --script res://../scripts/bake-seahorse.gd -- --study=<absolute private working directory>` to bundle the scene and produce its manifest. Place `sea-horse.scn` and `sea-horse.json` in `Viewer/local-art/mounts` and launch with the prepared Enhanced profile. The reconstruction's animated mesh and the private atlas are generated locally.


# Enhanced cattle

The optional cattle presentation covers cow bodies 216 and 231 and bull bodies 232 and 233. Each retains its Third Dawn rig, existing geometry, coat material and base gait tracks. Six fitted actions cover the fall, head recoil, short nudge, grazing, a tail flick and the raise to alert. The cow-tip packet retains the living lie-down and returns to grazing. The two bulls have separate body-specific fall trajectories. The official alternate bull selector points to standing frames, and the real death packet then starts the fitted fall.

The four named `AnimationSequence.uop` records are `0xf407df1a5e94dc5c`, `0xccb899bfb8d1529a`, `0x8170ad892018e264` and `0x9d39436ce1f1a38e` respectively. The official `AnimationFrame4.uop` supplied 1,590 registered AMOU frames in 36 members. Cow 216 group 24 advertises 35 frames and its validated member contains 40. Its fitted presentation retains the established running gait.

The twelve baked scenes passed 11,592 weighted pose checks and retained all 132 pre-existing clip instances. The sampled grazing hoof displacement stayed within 3.01 mm. The current scenes passed 240 Forward+ views. The finite terrain replay measured 4,104 skinned poses and 108 handoffs, with a maximum displayed joint displacement of 6.23 micrometres during continued falls. The normal client replay verified 434 Enhanced and 33 Classic selections and corpse checks. Classic now blends from the pose, facing and ground offset shown when the death packet arrives. The largest sampled head displacement at the handoff was 0.86 micrometres. The dedicated Classic replay passed 72 packet and corpse handoffs with 2,952 weighted pose samples.

Two disposable ModernUO clients passed 87 checks in Enhanced mode. Both cow bodies tipped and recovered alive, yielded four eligible milk servings and refused the fifth while depleted. All four native bodies walked, ran and retained raw server dye. Stock death packets produced fitted static corpses, and late partial dye kept their shape and placement. The fixture called the ordinary native APIs and sent the Enhanced alert and alternate selectors explicitly. The separate Classic run passed 518 checks across all eight body and sex combinations.

With the existing bovine actions prepared, rebuild using Godot's `scripts/bake-cattle-enhanced.gd`, then run `python -B scripts/install-cattle-enhanced.py`. `scripts/verify.ps1` checks the installed manifest, finite terrain and both modes. Local Third Dawn scenes, official sprite references and captures stay outside Git.


# Enhanced ordinary horses

The optional presentation covers ordinary horses 200, 204, 226 and 228. It retains the licensed Warhorse Lite rig, established rider sockets, coat materials and mounted gaits. Nine additional motions cover the two falls, rearing, recoil, short bound, head dip and quiet reset. The client selects the exact body and combat-mode action mapping. The tan and dapple-grey references share almost all registered silhouettes, as do the dark-brown and dun references. The first 3D fit shares the saddle rig and common motion curves across the four coats. The two reference pairs differ visibly in shape and timing, and the exact limb curves remain an art refinement.

The official Enhanced `AnimationFrame3.uop` and `AnimationSequence.uop` yielded 2,540 AMOU frames. The named sequence hashes are `0x86e9fff3f17efa4a` for 200, `0x3169a88ccc260b18` for 204, `0x797d7f9e5af48cdc` for 226 and `0xa1e2f9a8b3300b9` for 228. Bodies 200 and 226 have 13 source groups and bodies 204 and 228 have 12. The exact sources supplied the two separate fall poses and the variant-specific long rear. The horse rig and texture licence remains recorded in `scripts/horse-inputs.json`.

Local verification covered 10,247 action selections, 82 staged Enhanced and 46 Classic world replay checks, 90 rendered dapple-grey poses and 1,584 skinned fall poses on level and tilted physical platforms. The maximum sampled handoff displacement was 5.01 micrometres. The isolated two-client ModernUO trial passed 89 checks across the four native bodies, including stock mounting, walk, run, dismount, death and late partial dye. The fixture sent the Enhanced gesture and alternate fall packets explicitly. The ordinary stock death uses the fitted first fall. Both static cadavers keep their pose and placement when dyed.



# Enhanced Treefellow

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 152 bodies and 419 fitted local scenes. Treefellow 301 retains its original Third Dawn mesh, 63-joint rig and root toes. Fourteen motions follow 835 registered reference frames in 17 groups from sequence 0x5c4d9a10c6b2c4c7. Combat and peaceful idles are distinct. The peaceful walk preserves the original root and leg tracks. The modern material layers bark relief and green moss over the source atlas and applies the server's full or partial dye.

The first fall topples onto the ground. The other lowers the canopy and leaves the rooted trunk upright. Across the focused samples the upright frozen corpse was at least 1.68 times as tall as the toppled corpse. Forward+ rendered 170 poses. The dense check covered 11,508 samples at 240 Hz with no measured hand or forearm crossing of the torso. Focused tests passed 405 routes, 4,875 terrain poses, and 960 samples across 60 fall handoffs. The largest sampled displacement was 1.17 micrometres. The staged build passed 2,954 Classic and 3,736 Enhanced world replay checks. A late partial dye preserved both static corpse poses and positions in the replay.

The isolated native trial passed 32 checks. A stock swing selected Dismount, spent 20 mana and dealt 53 total damage in the recorded hit. Both clients saw the horse return, the rider's mount layer clear and the stock particles. A regular double-click was refused during the daze and succeeded after the block expired. The dyed native corpse contained the Treefellow's logs and gold. The fixture established aggression and invoked the ordinary weapon swing against a test rider.

Rebuild with scripts/bake-treefellow-enhanced.gd. Verify with scripts/render-treefellow.gd, scripts/check-treefellow-clearance.gd and scripts/test-treefellow-enhanced.gd. Install with python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --treefellow after reproducing the required local evidence.


# Enhanced Meer Mage

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 151 bodies and 416 fitted local scenes. Meer Mage 770 retains the original Third Dawn rig, robe, atlas and two falls. The official sequence 0x6d6f75a039140a6d identifies 235 registered frames across ten groups. Eight new motions include three distinct attacks, guard, recoil, idle, hip check and a measured walk using the original leg tracks. The Enhanced run slot points to standing frames, so the client keeps the original Third Dawn run at running speed.

Forward+ rendered 110 poses. At 240 Hz the clearance check sampled 4,113 poses without a measured hand or forearm crossing of the torso. The focused client run checked 402 routes, 3,000 terrain poses and 960 samples across 60 fall handoffs. The largest sampled handoff displacement was 0.82 micrometres. Both falls freeze to dyed static corpses.

The native insect call uses the retained spell cast. Both clients received the swarm effect and the torch dispersal cue. The renderer follows the affected character and clears on torch dispersal, death or expiry. The actual stock attack dealt 31 damage in the recorded trial. The player used a regular torch from the backpack. The forest call spawned stock enraged rabbit, hart, black bear and eagle bodies on the sampled terrain. Both clients received those animals and the dyed Meer Mage corpse. The test advanced the stock ability cooldown to exercise its two branches.

Rebuild with scripts/bake-meer-mage-enhanced.gd. Verify with scripts/render-meer-mage.gd, scripts/check-meer-mage-clearance.gd, scripts/test-meer-mage-enhanced.gd and scripts/test-meer-mage-swarm.gd. Install with python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --meer-mage after reproducing the required local evidence.


# Enhanced Giant Beetle

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 150 bodies and 413 fitted local scenes. Giant Beetle 791 keeps its Third Dawn carapace, atlas, six articulated legs and 51 original joints. The Enhanced sequence 0x8a7365d17b57b517 identifies 710 registered frames in 15 groups. Eight additional joints articulate the original wing covers and two authored hindwing membranes. The hidden abdomen uses the original texture atlas. The retained body remains closed for the three seated gaits and opens for the combat walk, idle, attacks and displays.

The three treatments preserve the original animation tracks and 5,460 sampled seat and sole transforms. Forward+ rendered 170 poses. Focused client checks covered 387 routes, 2,565 gait poses, 60 death handoffs and 60 frozen corpses across flat and sloped terrain. The largest sampled handoff displacement was 0.80 micrometres. The complete world replays passed 2,908 Classic and 3,677 Enhanced checks. A later dye preserved the world corpse's pose and placement. Enhanced type 3 selects a fall that folds the wings for its first variant. The source sends the combat gait for its alternate type 3 entry, so the client preserves the established fall on that branch.

The isolated two-client ModernUO trial passed 161 checks. Both riders mounted, walked, ran and dismounted with the exact layer 25 item 0x3EBC. Full and partial hues replicated. The owner opened the native strong backpack and both clients saw the native corpse. Four controlled Enhanced actions exercised the combat flags and wing motions on each rider's dismounted Beetle. The fitted rider review passed 145 checks across 36 poses.

Rebuild with scripts/bake-giant-beetle-enhanced.gd and verify with scripts/render-giant-beetle.gd and scripts/test-giant-beetle-enhanced.gd. Install with python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --giant-beetle after reproducing the required local evidence.


# Enhanced Oni

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 149 bodies and 410 fitted local scenes. Oni body 241 retains the original Third Dawn mesh, atlas, horns, claws, rig and established fall animations. Twelve fitted motions follow named sequence 0xab3fd539c59b1666. The inspected official archives supplied 675 registered frames in sixteen groups. The archive references group 27 in one combat gesture selector. Its frames remain unlocated, and that selector keeps the established high gesture.

The long double spiral from source group 12 is an authored, hand-tracked Enhanced cast effect. It follows the hand during the matching phase and disappears when the cast finishes or a fall begins. The stock Oni is a grounded MageAI creature. Its Angry Fire attack remains a server TODO.

The scenes passed 140 Forward+ views, 8,157 dense limb poses at 240 Hz, 492 route selections, 5,580 terrain poses and 960 fall samples over 60 handoffs. The measured forearms and hands cleared the torso. The largest sampled joint displacement at a fall handoff was 1.25 micrometres. Both falls pass the normal client replay and freeze into static corpses. A later partial hue update preserves their placement and geometry.

Two isolated clients passed 87 checks. They saw native movement, stock MageAI spell effects and damage at four tiles, the raw partial dye and the persistent corpse with gold. A separate controlled type 11 packet exercised the fitted hand flame on both clients.

Rebuild with scripts/bake-oni-enhanced.gd and verify with scripts/check-oni-clearance.gd, scripts/render-oni.gd and scripts/test-oni-enhanced.gd. Install with python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --oni after reproducing the required earlier batches and local evidence.


# Enhanced Orc Brute

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 148 bodies and 407 fitted local scenes. The original Third Dawn table assigns the Orc Brute body 189 the Orc Lord mesh and rig. The optional Brute keeps that rig, original animation keys and native size ratios. It has a broader torso and limbs, a shorter crest and authored skin-bound spines on the head, shoulders and back. The Orc Lord body 138 keeps its own established appearance.

Thirteen fitted motions follow named sequence 0xc04e4e5da697b64e, with 725 registered reference frames across 16 indexed groups. The separate peaceful walk keeps the original leg tracks. The source requests the low ready stance when peace changes to combat. Its spell action uses the double hammer motion. The original forward death remains available alongside the separate fitted backward death.

The fitted scenes passed 140 Forward+ views, 9,276 dense poses at 240 Hz with zero measured forearm or hand crossings of the torso, 492 action and gait selections, 5,580 supported terrain poses and 960 fall samples over 60 handoffs. The largest sampled handoff joint displacement was 2.48 micrometres. The paired world replays froze both falls with the spines attached and preserved the pose and placement after a late partial hue packet. The neutral, full hue and partial hue renders matched their frozen appearances. The partial dye produced a smaller measured colour change than the full dye.

Two isolated clients passed 40 checks with a disposable player casting Magic Arrow through the server's target cursor. Spell damage caused a stock body-138 Orcish Lord to target the caster. Ordinary damage caused no spawn. Both clients saw the summoned Lord die and its corpse disappear. The dyed Orc Brute corpse persisted with gold, 25 Shadow Iron Ore and 10 Iron Ingots.

Rebuild with scripts/bake-orc-brute-enhanced.gd and the --brute-silhouette option. Verify with scripts/check-orc-brute-clearance.gd, scripts/render-orc-brute.gd, scripts/render-orc-brute-hues.gd and scripts/test-orc-brute-enhanced.gd. Install with python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --orc-brute after reproducing the earlier required batches and local evidence.


# Enhanced Moloch

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 147 bodies and 404 fitted local scenes. Moloch body 785 retains the Third Dawn rig, mesh, atlas and established action clips. Fourteen fitted motions use registered references from named sequence 0x7dd809e7721b6576. The archive audit found 17 indexed groups and 785 frames. The new combat and peaceful stances move all four arms independently. The peaceful walk retains the source leg tracks. The prior combat walk, run and forward fall are reused.

The Enhanced death references have two different trajectories. Across all 40 registered frames of each group, the positioned silhouettes overlap by about 12 percent on average. The new alternate fall goes backward. The synthetic world replay transferred that pose to a static corpse and preserved the geometry and placement on a later dye update. The Classic replay retained its established single fall.

The prepared scenes passed 150 Forward+ views, 10,062 dense poses at 240 Hz, 492 route selections, 30 alternate-death handoffs and 480 terrain samples. The four measured forearms and hands cleared the torso. The smallest distance between the measured same-side forearm centre lines was 33.4 cm before arm thickness. The largest sampled handoff moved a traced joint by less than one micrometre.

An isolated two-client trial passed 36 checks against stock ModernUO. Concussion Blow dealt 10 extra damage to the target at balanced health and mana percentages and 30 to the imbalanced target. Each trial after the stock cooldown consumed 25 mana. Ordinary hits from a Moloch with zero mana dealt damage without concussion. Both clients saw the stock effects on the victim. The trial also verified partial dye, the body-785 corpse with gold and resistance to regular poison.

Rebuild with scripts/bake-moloch-enhanced.gd and verify with scripts/check-moloch-clearance.gd, scripts/render-moloch.gd and scripts/test-moloch-enhanced.gd. Install with python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --moloch after reproducing the earlier required batches and local evidence.


# Enhanced Crystal Lattice Seeker

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 146 bodies and 401 fitted local scenes. Crystal Lattice Seeker 123 retains its original 40-bone Third Dawn rig, mesh and atlas. Named sequence 0x2b773a8ac8564a09 points to 830 decoded reference frames across 17 indexed groups. Thirteen new motions include the separate peaceful walk, wing cocoon and unfurl, recoil, cast and forward alternate fall. The peaceful walk preserves the earlier leg tracks. Four groups reuse the established combat walk, idle, run and first fall.

The fitted strikes and cast show a short authored spectral arc at the striking hand. Three additional flashes follow the stock Seeker's localized mana, health and stamina drain notices. Their colours are client presentation choices. The stock Seeker remains an unarmed MageAI creature.

Both isolated clients received all three actual stat transfers, localized notices and matching sound effects while the Seeker both dealt and took melee damage. The fixture invoked the stock unarmed weapon swing against a disposable target and reset stats between bounded attempts. The final 116 checks also covered the stock body, hue 0x47e, one-tile move, partial hue and gold in the corpse.

The fitted scenes passed 130 Forward+ views, 492 focused route checks, 3,570 terrain poses and 4,320 fall samples over 360 handoffs. The largest measured handoff at the sampled head, hand and wing joints was 1.44 micrometres. The synthetic world suite carried the alternate fall into a skeleton-free corpse and preserved its pose and placement on a later dye packet. Classic group 3 retains its established fall.

Rebuild with scripts/bake-crystal-lattice-seeker-enhanced.gd. Verify with scripts/render-crystal-lattice-seeker.gd and scripts/test-crystal-lattice-seeker-enhanced.gd. Install with python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --crystal-lattice-seeker after reproducing the required earlier batches and local evidence.


# Enhanced Gargoyle Destroyer and Enforcer

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 145 bodies and 398 fitted local scenes. The Destroyer 755 and Enforcer 754 retain their individual Third Dawn rigs, armour atlases and wing meshes. Each uses 795 decoded frames in 17 indexed groups. Named sequences 0xeb16abd2efa90f38 and 0x7c84bf0bf87ed6b1 select distinct combat and peaceful walks, with flight inheriting the peaceful glance. The same stock attacks stay available in Classic mode. An additional alternate fall follows Enhanced group 3. The existing Classic group 21 still selects the folded-wing pose. The fitted peaceful walk preserves the original leg tracks and adjusts the torso, arms and wings.

The installed candidate passed 2,034 routing and effect checks. The 240 Forward+ views and 16,164 dense samples cover both bodies and all fitted clips. The measured forearms and hands cleared the torso. The supported foot gaps for the non-fall clips were at most 3 mm. The local world fixture passed 78 Classic and 119 Enhanced checks with Fire Gargoyle and Shadow Wisp enabled in the batch. Both alternate falls continued into skeleton-free cadavers. The greatest observed discontinuity at the sampled joints was 1.17 micrometres. A subsequent dye packet preserved each cadaver's geometry and placement.

The two-client isolated ModernUO trial passed 94 checks. A real stock bow swing prompted the Destroyer's moving hatchet and counter damage. Unarmed swings damaged the Destroyer without a hatchet reply. The visual projectile started at the displayed right grip and uses project-authored geometry. The stock Enforcer hit both adjacent test targets with Whirlwind and spent 15 mana. Its fixed graphic starts the new turning sweep and a short ring. Both stock creatures moved one tile and produced dyed corpses with gold. The fixture invoked the stock weapon swings against disposable targets. No server code changes were required.

Rebuild with scripts/bake-gargoyle-destroyer-enhanced.gd. Verify with scripts/check-gargoyle-destroyer-clearance.gd, scripts/render-gargoyle-destroyer.gd and scripts/test-armored-gargoyles-enhanced.gd. Install with python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --armored-gargoyles after reproducing the earlier required batches and local evidence.


# Enhanced Shadow Wisp

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 143 bodies and 392 fitted local scenes. Body 165 keeps the project-owned Wisp rig and its 16 retained clips. Named Enhanced sequence 0xaa70d35798ac1dd5 points to 276 registered frames across five groups. All weapon subactions select the same arc and all locomotion slots select the same hover cycle. The new recoil and kindling fit groups 10 and 23. Group 10 has five inferred directional strips of eleven frames and a distinct trailing neutral frame whose exact use remains open.

The optional material responds to the attack, recoil and emergence while retaining the wild and familiar hues. The source-based arc curls around the core, extends forward and fades. A dying Wisp blends its displayed pose into the retained fall and freezes at the measured height. Transient sparks clear at the handoff. Forward+ rendered 30 views. Focused tests passed 186 checks including all three hue modes and 18 cadavers. A finite-slope trial passed 620 hover poses. The staged Britain replays passed 418 Classic and 561 Enhanced checks.

Both native forms passed 148 isolated server checks. The stock Summon Familiar spell assigned hue 0x901 and one follower slot. Both clients received the periodic effect on the familiar and eligible caster. The caster gained eight mana and the positive-karma observer gained zero during the pulse. Releasing the familiar cleared the follower slot without a death packet. The wild Shadow Wisp used stock MageAI, accepted the controlled fitted action packets, and left a partially dyed corpse with its native bone loot.

The combined world runner removes finished fixture mobiles and corpses through the ordinary removal packet after each suite has asserted its results. The staged replays retained their check totals. In this workstation sample the Enhanced run fell from about 194 seconds to 148 seconds and the Classic run from 133 seconds to 130 seconds.

Rebuild with scripts/bake-shadow-wisp-enhanced.gd, then run scripts/check-shadow-wisp-clearance.gd, scripts/render-shadow-wisp.gd and scripts/test-shadow-wisp-enhanced.gd. Install with python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --shadow-wisp after reproducing the earlier required batches and local evidence.


# Enhanced Fire Gargoyle

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 142 bodies and 391 fitted local scenes. Fire Gargoyle body 130 keeps its Third Dawn rig, wings and base animation library. The named Enhanced sequence 0xd83f64ba26d623e2 has 690 frames across 16 indexed groups. New motions fit the two exact attack variants and ash search. With the combatants kept outside melee range during breath cooldown, the stock mage cast a spell and dealt ranged damage. Both clients received the native dyed charred corpse with gold. Controlled Enhanced actions are recorded separately from stock combat.

Rebuild with scripts/bake-fire-gargoyle-enhanced.gd, then run scripts/check-fire-gargoyle-clearance.gd, scripts/render-fire-gargoyle.gd and scripts/test-fire-gargoyle-enhanced.gd. # Enhanced Hydra and Abscess

The optional Enhanced profile reaches 141 bodies and 388 fitted local scenes. Hydra and Abscess share body 265 and the retained Third Dawn five-headed rig, mesh and atlas. Ten fitted motions follow the named Enhanced sequence 0x1e91857b69064898, with 865 frames across 17 indexed groups. Rebuild using scripts/bake-hydra-enhanced.gd and verify with scripts/check-hydra-clearance.gd, scripts/render-hydra.gd, scripts/render-hydra-hues.gd and scripts/test-hydra-enhanced.gd. The focused Enhanced suites run in up to four isolated Godot processes. The verifier preserves family order in its report and still runs both complete world replays after the focused suites. Six representative suites passed in 95 seconds with one worker and 34 seconds with four on this workstation.

The blades cleared 12,114 centreline samples across the three treatments. Terathan Drone 71 and Warrior 70 retain separate Third Dawn rigs and pincer movements. Their five fits each cover a low watch, pincer brace, forward alert, raised pincers and unfolding.

The three packages passed 5,580 terrain poses, 360 fitted falls, 30 retained Ice falls and 120 rendered views. Both the fixture and prepared Britain replayed the new actions and the matching static corpses. The isolated two-client ModernUO run passed 286 checks. The Drone and Warrior used their stock melee AI and the Ice Elemental dealt native first-circle Magic Arrow damage with a controlled target. The trials exercised movement, hues, loot, corpse carving and controlled Enhanced action packets. Normal startup was verified using the staged manifest and exact scene paths.

Rebuild with `scripts/bake-ice-elemental-enhanced.gd` and `scripts/bake-terathan-drone-warrior-enhanced.gd`. Install using `python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --ice-terathan`. The original art, fitted scenes and trial outputs remain local.

# Enhanced Ratman Mage, Lava Serpent, Cyclops and Titan

The optional profile reaches 91 bodies and 238 local fitted scenes. Twenty fitted motions use 985 registered frames from twenty named official members. All four creatures retain their earlier Third Dawn rigs, materials and action keys. The Ratman Mage switches to an original 92-triangle crook in seven fitted motions and keeps it through the fall, including late dye changes to the corpse. The crook cleared 18,900 samples. The Lava Serpent rises 1.72 metres from its emerging pose and has a separate authored Fire Breath wind-up with a mouth origin for the stock projectile. The Cyclops keeps its original mallet through all five new actions and clears 9,501 prop samples. The Titan uses its own unarmed gestures.

Across all four bodies, the fitted and Fire Breath poses passed 9,765 terrain samples and 630 interrupted falls. The fixture and prepared Britain each passed 121 Classic and 169 Enhanced world checks for these four bodies. Vulkan rendered 220 sampled views. Two disposable ModernUO clients verified stock movement, combat, hues, loot and corpses. The server's Fire Breath dealt timed damage, the Ratman Mage and Titan cast native Lightning with controlled targets, and the Cyclops used its stock melee AI. Controlled Enhanced action packets exercised the new client routes separately.

Recreate the crook inputs with `python -B scripts/prepare-ratman-mage-crook.py <local Models directory>`. The pinned members are `Others/weapon_crook_lod2.grn` and `Maps/weapons_512.tga`. Rebuild using `bake-ratman-mage-enhanced.gd`, `bake-lava-serpent-enhanced.gd` and `bake-cyclops-titan-enhanced.gd`, and install with `python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --ratman-mage-lava-serpent --cyclops-titan`. Original art, fitted scenes and render outputs stay in the prepared local workspace.

The red Dragon yielded seven red scales and at least 20 barbed hides. The Warrior dropped its axe and gourd.

Rebuild with `scripts/bake-yomotsu-warrior-dragon-enhanced.gd`. Install using `python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --yomotsu-warrior-dragon`.

# Enhanced Skeletal Knight, Acid Elemental, Pixie and Frost Troll

Thirteen fitted motions use 575 registered 2D reference frames from thirteen official AMOU members. The optional profile reaches 79 bodies and 202 local fitted scenes. The default action ledger remains at 6,489 of 8,090 tracked slots.

Skeletal Knight body 147 retains the Third Dawn rig, scimitar and wooden shield. Both props stay in their grips during the two new living motions. During the fall the shield releases along a continuous path and settles about 12 mm above the flat plane in the bake. The scimitar stays attached to its hand. The five new motions use bare hands as in the Enhanced references. The held corpse pose stays unarmed if the server sends a new colour.

Rebuild with `scripts/bake-skeletal-knight-enhanced.gd`, `scripts/bake-acid-elemental-enhanced.gd` and `scripts/bake-pixie-frost-troll-enhanced.gd`. The optional profile verifier checks the named sequence records, individual frame payloads, candidate scene hashes and isolated native receipts. Install using `python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --knight-acid-pixie-frost`. Original inputs, fitted scenes and disposable test data stay local.

Fire ignition also yields to Enhanced alert, Classic melee and walking. Both isolated clients received the stock melee actions, hue changes and native deaths. The trial passed 271 checks across Crane, Fire Elemental and the real Fire Elemental summon, including 60 controlled Enhanced packets. The Crane supplies 25 feathers and both Fire forms carry the native light item.

The summon sends a death packet with a real corpse serial before deleting the temporary item. The client drops its pending snapshot on that removal, even if the world-item packet never arrived. The fall can finish after the mobile is removed. Normal Crane and wild Fire corpses retain their final pose. The targeted world replay passed 36 Classic and 48 Enhanced checks.

Rebuild with `scripts/bake-crane-fire-enhanced.gd` and validate with `python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --crane-fire`. Original sprites, derived scenes, renders and native test receipts stay local.

The pinned server's ordinary creature gameplay does not currently emit them.

Rebuild the local candidates with `scripts/bake-orc-enhanced.gd` and `scripts/bake-water-enhanced.gd`. Run `scripts/test-ranked-enhanced.gd` and the private two-client recipe in `work/creatures/native-ranked-actions/run.ps1`. Use `scripts/uo-enhanced-frames.py --input <local-source-folder> --output <local-review-folder> --sheets 17 16` for the frame audit. To install the extended profile, run `python -B scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install --ranked-source <local-source-folder>` with the pinned `AnimationSequence.uop` and extracted `ec-frame-<body>-<group>.bin` files. The pinned member digests are in `scripts/prepare-ranked-enhanced-actions.py`. The generated assets and receipts stay local. The default ledger remains 6,489 of 8,090.

# Native fishing-net encounters

One regular and one fabled fishing net produced all five stock appearances through ordinary targeting and the native net timer. Sea Serpent, Deep Sea Serpent, Water Elemental, Kraken and Leviathan had the expected body, hue and swimming flags on the disposable server. Both clients selected the correct appearance and received the matching native corpse after controlled kills. The fabled net bound Leviathan to the casting player. One Sea Serpent action was decoded by both clients. The 87-check trial leaves autonomous combat and rewards open. The isolated fixture placed the boat and two test characters for the casts. Local reproduction and receipts are under `work/creatures/native-fishing-audit`.

# Optional Enhanced creature actions and native ore elementals

The optional Enhanced creature profile selects 14 original fidget records across seven bodies and the Troglodyte alert. It uses the actor's native flags and the packet variation to select from the ordered source groups. The source handler tries the action's subaction, then its wildcard, then the mode fallback. The prepared records all use wildcard subactions. This profile uses the original selector's unrestricted setting for variation bytes 0 through 127. The default client keeps the existing Classic-compatible routes.

The two Troglodyte fidgets and the Enslaved Gargoyle's quiet gesture were fitted to the retained rigs and tested across all three treatments. The other source groups use already verified clips. The package passed 243 decoded visual selections and 810 compatibility checks. The main world scene passed six dispatch checks in each profile. Two disposable clients passed 91 checks on native Troglodyte and Lurg with real peaceful and combat states. The isolated fixture emitted the comparison packets. Separately, stock AI sent Classic idle groups 17 and 18 to both clients in the 21-check idle trial.

Build the two scene sets with `scripts/bake-troglodyte-fidgets.gd` and `scripts/bake-gargoyle-quiet-fidget.gd`, check them with `scripts/test-enhanced-fidget-candidates.gd`, and run the isolated `work/creatures/native-form-audit/run.ps1 -EnhancedActions` comparison. Run `scripts/prepare-enhanced-creature-actions.py --install` to prepare the local profile. The profile is enabled by `./launch.ps1 -EnhancedCreatureActions` or the Godot user argument `--enhanced-creature-actions`. The client requires the matching base art and fitted scenes before it enables the profile. Generated scenes and game-derived data stay local.

The cached official patch indexes match 392 distinct body and frame-group pairs and all choices in 406 unresolved action records. Directly fetched samples confirm 33 AMOU sprite files and 1,595 registered frame headers across nine high-occurrence bodies. The header and palette structure is verified. Pixel decoding and scene fitting remain separate work. The 6,489 of 8,090 figure still describes the default packet routes. [Official Ultima Online download page](https://uo.com/client-download/)

All eight ore elementals passed 150 checks through actual Gargoyle's Pickaxe use and native mining. The disposable fixture supplied a skilled miner, selected each starting vein in a fresh test bank, and repositioned the miner for line of sight after spawn. Stock mining produced each elemental with its exact body and hue, a native combat target and 25 ore. Both clients saw a melee action for seven tiers and Dull Copper's hit reaction. Controlled kills produced eight matching native corpse records and static 3D cadavers. Evidence and reproduction are under `work/creatures/native-form-audit/run.ps1 -OreElementals`.

# Troglodyte and Lurg locomotion

Troglodyte and Lurg now crouch at rest, lower their arms for a peaceful walk and use a longer, forward-leaning sprint. The fitted loops use the recovered rig and reference groups 22, 24 and 25 from AnimationFrame1.uop. The closest repeated interval of the recovered walk spans samples 412 to 536 at 120 Hz. A short correction closes the new loops while every previous animation key stays intact. The source graph assigns group 28 to the peace-to-combat transition. The client plays the retained reach when that flag changes and gives incoming attacks and deaths priority. Classic-compatible type 8 continues to select Pillage.

All three model treatments passed 1,647 flat and sloped support samples and three static corpse hues. The largest measured loop seam was 0.054 mm, and both idle soles stayed within 3.3 mm of flat ground. The sprint's peak fore-aft foot separation increased by about 28% in the modern treatment. The retained packet regression passed 36 selections and 1,674 terrain poses. The combined Quagmire-through-Exodus regression passed 468 Classic and 15 newer selections, 9,207 terrain samples and 36 corpse hues. A private Vulkan comparison rendered 54 poses. Two clients passed 63 isolated native checks using normal appearance and attack updates for Troglodyte and Lurg, including the same-serial return to peace and Lurg's hue 1109. This trial proves the native stance transition. The walk and sprint use the client motion path and were tested separately from the live stance sequence.

Run `scripts/bake-troglodyte-gaits.gd` and `scripts/test-troglodyte-gaits.gd` to build and test the candidates. The isolated `work/creatures/native-form-audit/run.ps1 -TroglodyteGaits` trial seals the native evidence before `scripts/accept-troglodyte-gaits.py --activate` installs the local scenes. The packet-action ledger remains at 6,489 of 8,090 slots because the new clips are selected by movement and state.

# Centaur, Twaulo and Doom skin bindings

The Centaur, Shadow Knight and Ravager now use the skin table belonging to each visible mesh in their original Granny files. The old conversion included two Centaur helper tables or one Doom weapon table before the body's own table. This sent vertices to the wrong joints even though the original bind pose and packet playback passed. The Centaur uses the third table's 41 bindings. The Shadow Knight uses its 43-entry body table and the Ravager its 50-entry table. Their original rigid sword and club retain their own bindings.

A scan of 256 local creature models measured the original and corrected poses against each model's own skinned bind pose. Across idle, walk and run, the worst sampled 95th-percentile edge stretch fell from 3.39Ã— to 1.32Ã— for Centaur, 5.98Ã— to 1.20Ã— for Shadow Knight and 4.72Ã— to 1.28Ã— for Ravager. Offscreen renders verified all three corrections. The original animation curves remain except for the model-space grounding track, which is measured from the corrected skin. The largest adjustment was 0.474 metres during the Centaur's original attack. The server's position stays authoritative.

Centaur and Twaulo use the original 92-triangle Third Dawn bow when the server sends its worn item. The bow follows the human wrist and fingertips, and the brace uses the rig's human arms. Both clients verified the native bow, partial dye, removal, re-equip, movement and the existing static corpse path. The fitted bow covers carrying and equipment display. ModernUO sends a projectile when the Centaur fires and omits the monster bow swing animation, so a separate client release remains open.

The exact isolated runs passed 372 Centaur and Twaulo checks and 348 Shadow Knight and Ravager checks. The Centaur passed 600 full-skin and bow pose samples, 279 finite slope samples and three corpse hues. The full Doom playback suite passed 105 decoded selections, 5,022 terrain samples and 27 corpse hue cases with the two corrected models. The earlier action and corpse recipes retain their original evidence.

Rebuild the local models with `scripts/prepare-centaur-mesh-binding.py`, `scripts/prepare-centaur-bow.py` and `scripts/prepare-doom-mesh-bindings.py`. The corresponding Godot bake scripts keep the original rig and recompute the visual floor track. `scripts/accept-creature-mesh-bindings.py` checks the isolated client and server receipts before installing the corrections. Game art and review evidence stay local.

# Ridgebacks through dragons, Gargoyle flight and Tormented Minotaur blocks

Seven bodies now route 94 directly stored Classic groups on their retained rigs and materials. The Skeletal Dragon's seven-frame Classic flight-death group uses a held wing pose distinct from its ordinary collapse and static cadaver.

The mounted trial passed 370 checks across both Ridgebacks and Reptalon, including worn mount items, all seven rider actions, raw hues, movement, dismount, rest and native corpses. Reptalon reported flight capability and stayed grounded throughout the native trial. The dense Reptalon fit also passed 1,046 checks across 252 sampled rider poses for human and elf rigs. Offline playback passed 282 Classic selections, 4,743 terrain samples and 21 static corpse hue cases.

Tormented Minotaur routes Classic groups 15 and 16 to its recovered right and left blocks. Newer type 2 selects the left block for even packet modes and the right block for odd modes. The three bodies passed 695 isolated checks, 18 offline selections and nine static corpse hue cases. The retained Frost and Tormented Minotaur packages passed their older route and skin checks. Rebuild with `scripts/bake-gargoyle-flight-minotaur-actions.gd` and `scripts/prepare-gargoyle-flight-minotaur-actions.py`. The retained Savage Rider profiles passed 222 normal client checks and 438 isolated server checks with the new Ridgeback renderer. The server trial covered both rider bodies, the native mount item, movement, dismount and remount, and the rider corpse after the server removes its mount.

The generic ledger routes 5,564 of 7,131 slots (78.0%) and leaves 1,567 broader gaps. This batch replaces shared or generic clips inside existing routes and adds one newer route. Its rig matches all 32 Grey Wolf joint names, parents and rest transforms. The client uses four of the already fitted Timber Wolf clips and retains the Grey Wolf source keys. A vertical correction to the recovered strike resolves about 108 mm of floor penetration in the nominal flat sample. The retained fidget can still dip about 10 mm.

Grizzly Bear 212, Boar 290 and Goat 209 now have distinct Classic strikes, fidgets and collapses. Grizzly has an upright swipe and a lower strike. Boar drops onto its belly with tucked hooves or rolls onto its side. Goat has two opposite falls. The fitted motions approximate the original views. Their source meshes and animation keys are retained.

The isolated ModernUO run passed 1,047 checks across both clients and all five native bodies. It used each real constructor, including both random Grey Wolf outcomes, and verified controlled action requests, native movement, raw hues, death packets and replicated corpses. The active client passed 8,019 decoded poses, fifteen static corpse hue cases and fifteen decoded death and removal handoffs across 615 terrain samples. The catalogue still covers 324 static corpse body keys.

This release reduced generic Classic action pairs sharing a replacement from 818 to 803. It routed 5,334 of 6,924 tracked slots (77.0%). The combined project regression for this batch and the cattle loading fix passed, including the 4,096 terrain and 2,689 static samples in the world check.

# Cow and Bull actions

Both Cow bodies (216 and 231) and both Bull bodies (232 and 233) now have separate Classic strikes, fidgets and collapses on their own Third Dawn rigs. The Classic catalogue records 44 stored body and action combinations from `anim.mul`, with all five directions checked for each. The twelve formerly shared pairs now play distinct clips. The three fitted replacements per body approximate the Classic poses. The recovered Third Dawn animation keys are preserved. Both cow coats still use the exact living tip signature and return to standing.

The isolated ModernUO trial passed 518 checks. Both clients received the requested Classic strike and fidget. Native death and removal packets took each body through its default collapse and through a second run that continued the alternate collapse already playing. The replicated static corpses retained body ID, partial hue and the settled pose. The trial also exercised normal movement packets, mount refusal and all four owner milk servings before exhaustion. It used the server's milking API and controlled action requests. Autonomous AI and client beverage-item interaction remain outside that evidence.

The client passed 4,680 pose checks across 12 body and mesh combinations. Its separate decoded packet test passed 72 death, removal and static corpse handoffs across flat ground and two slopes, with 2,952 terrain poses. The static endpoint sat about 2 mm above the tested plane. The source Death dips by up to 29 mm on flat ground before correction. The bovine catalogue is counted separately from the generic 77.0% action ledger.

The first full cattle regression stopped after an unrelated source-family test logged Godot resource leaks at exit. Loading the cattle renderer only when the animal registry selects cattle removed the teardown errors. All 197 source-family skin checks then passed, and the cattle batch repeated all 518 isolated ModernUO checks and its active client gates with the corrected loading path.

# Giant Spider and Mongbat actions

Giant Spider 28 now has distinct strikes, recoils, a fidget and a second collapse on its retained Third Dawn rig. The alternate collapse uses the original fall with separately fitted front legs and abdomen. Its final visual displacement is about 0.54 m, down from 1.85 m in the recovered fall, with the native root held fixed. The new grounded actions approximate the Classic frames.

Mongbat 39 now uses its original Third Dawn airborne death. The source matched 55 motion tracks on the retained 58-joint rig, and the imported clip carries 65 tracks. The bake adds vertical clearance. On the nominal flat test the skin starts 1.17 m above the floor. The client selects the airborne fall if the last action was flight, takeoff or landing, and continues from the current phase if the corpse packet arrives during the fall. The grounded collapses remain separate. Both creatures retain their default static corpses, with the displayed endpoint carried into the static item when a transition runs.

The active gate passed 4,875 decoded poses across six body and mesh combinations, six static corpse hue checks and 738 terrain samples on flat ground and two slopes. The terrain check includes twelve decoded death, removal and static corpse handoffs plus six explicit airborne collapses. A private Forward+ review covered 44 views. The isolated ModernUO trial passed 1,117 checks with two clients across Giant Spider and the three Mongbat constructors. It exercised native grounded deaths and controlled requests for the explicit airborne collapse and newer WingSettle. The death packet arriving mid-flight was exercised with decoded client packets. Autonomous flight and a native mid-flight death remain unverified.

The ledger now routes 5,334 of 6,924 tracked slots (77.0%). It has 818 distinct Classic action pairs sharing a replacement, down from 835. The static corpse catalogue covers 324 body keys.

# Ratman, Brown Bear, Panther and Cougar actions

Ratman 42 now has two distinct collapses, separate strikes, reactions and a head-and-tail fidget on its original rig. The backward collapse settles the torso near the ground. The retained forward collapse and the Classic group-11 Hunch preserve their original keys. Both clients also played Hunch for the newer type-14, subaction-0, variation-0 packet.

Brown Bear 167 keeps its own Third Dawn model. The installed mobile and corpse definitions point to Black Bear 211 for its Classic animation reference. Panther 214 and Cougar 63 keep their original models and materials and use Panther's Classic frames to guide the rear swipe, roll, stretch and sniff. Cougar's installed mobile and corpse definitions both point to Panther. Brown Bear and Cougar retain their native identities and colours. Panther retains its native hue 2305 before any test dye.

All four active bodies passed 873 isolated ModernUO checks with two clients. The installed check passed 6,762 decoded poses across all 12 body and mesh treatments and 12 decoded death, removal and static corpse handoffs over 492 terrain samples. The fitted clips approximate the original frames. The recovered Ratman and feline collapses require terrain correction and the static corpses preserve the settled endpoint.

That release routed 5,333 of 6,923 tracked slots (77.0%). It reduced distinct Classic groups sharing a replacement from 846 to 835. The earlier full run stopped on a test array error introduced while adding these bodies. The corrected full regression passed, including 71 server and 84 client development-edit checks, and the world check sampled 4,096 terrain points and 2,689 statics. The native launcher now fails if Godot reports a script error even when its process exits successfully.

The previous Timber Wolf, sheep, Great Hart and Skeleton release passed the full regression. The installed `Body.def` and `Corpse.def` add 320 scoped redirect candidates to the local catalogue. For 133 of those records, the catalogue has indexed frames for the target and none for the source. This is separate from the direct and Bodyconv frame counts and leaves runtime precedence open. Both definitions point Brown Bear 167 to Black Bear 211 and Cougar 63 to Panther 214. The next previews keep the original Third Dawn shapes and the server's native identities.

# Restless Soul mounted routes

The mounted renderer uses the native layer 25 graphic, serial and hue to show the split robe and saddle only while a supported mount is worn. Dismount restores the original on-foot visual. The seven Classic routes use six retained seated donors and one urging gesture fitted from the five stored views. The original and rounded robes retained the ghost atlas and 40-joint rig. A separate rebuild matched the geometry, atlas, binds and 31,818 animation keys. The packed files differed, so the isolated receipt keeps the exact tested scene hashes.

The actual client passed 1,575 decoded saddle poses across three treatments and three mount graphics. Forward+ captured 14 poses using a dyed horse and an ostard. The two-client ModernUO fixture passed 384 checks across Spectral Armour and Restless Soul. It used the server's mount setter and checked the worn item, independent dye, native walk and run, dismount, remount, and the seven exact Classic packets. Native death released the same live horse and both clients started the on-foot ghost collapse and received its corpse. This is an explicit fixture mount and does not establish autonomous riding. The tested saddle points and ankle positions cover the sampled poses. Continuous cloth and mount surface contact remain open.

The seven mounted routes are active locally. The batch brought the ledger to 5,328 of 6,918 slots (77.0%) with zero unresolved gaps in the Classic MUL source-frame audit. The combined regression passed before activation, and the mounted and on-foot checks and full project regression passed against the mounted registry.

Run `scripts/prepare-restless-soul.ps1 -Mounted` and `scripts/test-horses.ps1 -Live -SkipContact -CreatureFamily restless_soul_mounted` for the isolated trial. Use `python -B scripts/accept-restless-soul-mounted.py check-active` to verify the installed routes. The mounted bake preserves any scene already in the active registry.

# Restless Soul motion reconstruction

The retained archive and the existing ghost rig provided 42 comparable donor clips, including seven seated candidates. A five-view mask comparison identified usable starting poses and showed that several donor names did not predict a faithful match. The 17 previously missing on-foot slots now have distinct prepared clips. The fitted replacements use original donor tracks, registered root placement and facing, and targeted posing for the bow, cast, turn and other gestures. Sixteen improve average silhouette overlap against their respective donor or idle baselines. The chest fidget matches the reference less closely than idle by 0.009 overlap, while adding the separate chest gesture. The fits remain approximations. The directed cast improved from 0.495 to 0.605 mean overlap and the bow from 0.471 to 0.556.

The on-foot package passed 2,499 decoded skin poses across the three treatments, preserved all 27 earlier clip instances and passed three finite terrain death cases in both the candidate and active registry. Forward+ rendered 51 poses. The disposable two-client trial passed 388 checks across Spectral Armour and Restless Soul, including exact packets and playback for all 17 new ghost slots. Those routes are now active locally. The ledger routes 5,321 of 6,918 slots (76.9%) and leaves seven Classic gaps with stored frames. The corpse catalogue still covers 324 bodies. The full Spectral Armour regression passed and the combined registry regression is running.

The pinned donor library, three fitted scenes and preparation receipt regenerate byte for byte. Run `scripts/prepare-restless-soul.ps1` with the retained source files and an optional local `-Extractor` for the reviewed Unshield CLI, then `scripts/test-horses.ps1 -Live -SkipContact -CreatureFamily restless_soul_actions`. Use `python -B scripts/accept-restless-soul-actions.py check-active` to recheck the active routes.

The initial seated proof kept the source atlas and leg rig and split the lower robe into separately weighted panels. It improved silhouette overlap for all six seated donor trials. The seventh proof uses a separately fitted urging gesture. These proofs preceded the mounted routes documented above.

# Spectral Armour actions and Restless Soul trials

Spectral Armour 637 now has 26 active routes for the previously empty Classic action slots. Restless Soul 970 has nine staged donor motions on its 40-joint rig. The actual client sampled 5,145 poses across both bodies and all three treatments. The Spectral Armour alternate death applies terrain clearance to the rendered blade while preserving its native position. The closest tested blade point stayed 10 mm above nominal flat ground in the raw animation pass. The deepest tested ghost point was 5 mm below nominal ground after calibrating the original mesh separately from the rounded mesh. Forward+ rendered 53 candidate poses. The comparison with Classic frames exposed discrepancies in the ghost turn and bow. Keep the nine candidates outside active coverage while reviewing the remaining gestures.

The disposable two-client ModernUO fixture passed 340 checks for the 35 proposed routes, including exact Classic action IDs and frame counts. The trial also retained constructor hues, movement, Spectral Armour equipment and corpse suppression, and Restless Soul corpse replication. The fixture requests explicit Classic actions. Autonomous selection, modern packet subactions, continuous terrain and the additional constructors remain separate. The normal registry passed 3,822 decoded Spectral Armour poses, retained 21 named clip instances and passed three finite terrain death cases. The regenerated ledger routes 5,304 of 6,918 slots (76.7%) and leaves 24 Classic gaps with stored frames. The corpse catalogue covers 324 bodies. The preceding full project regression passed and the Spectral Armour regression is running.

Regenerate the local scimitar fit with `scripts/fit-spectral-landmarks.py`, `scripts/fit-spectral-silhouettes.py` and `scripts/bake-spectral-reconstruction.gd`. Build the private scenes using `scripts/bake-khaldun-actions.gd` and `scripts/prepare-khaldun-actions.py`. Run `scripts/test-khaldun-actions.gd` with the project Godot build and `scripts/test-horses.ps1 -Live -SkipContact -CreatureFamily khaldun_actions` for the isolated server trial. Use `python -B scripts/accept-khaldun-actions.py check-active` to recheck the active Spectral Armour route. The local review folder holds the ghost donor trials, captures and runtime logs. A second strict audit matched the Bow Attack, Combat Advance and one-handed Combat Idle donors after separate indexed scene-leaf checks. Crossbow and two-handed Combat Idle still reject the single-leaf repair.

# Five Classic creature actions and shared replacement audit

Skittering Hopper 302, Horde Demon 776, Devourer 303, Rikktor 172 and Shadow Knight 311 have individual Classic group 11 actions. Hopper uses a shortened recovered Fidget. Horde holds its idle stance while opening the source jaw. Devourer opens and closes the torso from the first part of its recovered Fidget. Rikktor combines the recovered tail and wing motion with a held idle pose. Shadow Knight glances and moves its free arm while holding the original sword grip. The recipes verify five original frame directions, the recovered donor files and the three saved model treatments. Existing tracks, materials and static corpse geometry are preserved.

The two isolated ModernUO trials passed 980 checks across both clients and the six native constructors. Local decoded checks covered 45 terrain runs, 3,285 sampled poses and 15 static corpse hue cases. Four creatures penetrated the sampled planes by at most 7.6 mm. On one Rikktor slope the existing idle foot adjustment penetrated about 58.5 mm and the new action added less than 0.25 mm. That inherited slope issue remains open. The native trials request actions explicitly and leave autonomous behaviour for separate verification.

The full regression passed on the stable pre-activation registry. Both batches then passed local activation and their original package route checks. At that checkpoint the ledger routed 5,252 of 6,840 protocol slots (76.8%). Distinct motions sharing one route and unresolved modern protocol modes are tracked separately. The post-activation run passed its creature checks. Windows Application Control blocked the developer-edit test executable during launch, including on direct retry. The 84 client developer-edit checks and world startup passed separately. The five-action source commit is `99b3be2` on GitHub.

Bake with Godot `--headless --path Viewer --script res://../scripts/bake-hopper-horde-actions.gd` and repeat with `bake-late-classic-actions.gd`. Run `./scripts/test-horses.ps1 -Live -SkipContact -CreatureFamily hopper_horde_actions` and repeat with `late_classic_actions`. Run `python -B scripts/accept-classic-gestures.py --batch hopper_horde_actions --check-active` and repeat with `late_classic_actions` to verify the local activation.
