- Gap: the Trinsic streaming route test was stopped by Claude Code's memory guard before finishing; to be rerun.

## 31 October 2026 - Distance haze and the compression pass (docs/COMPACT_STREAMING.md)

- Haze (owner: the horizon was too bright and showed low-detail distant geometry): LightingRig depth fog now starts at 35 m and closes by 420 m (was 70-700 m), takes more of the sky's colour (aerial perspective 0.8, sky affect 0.45), uses a darker blue-grey with a soft sun glow, and the horizon sky is less bright. Same depth fog, no extra cost. Before/after renders over Britain: distant flat patches now dissolve into air while the city stays crisp.
- Compression, measured first: the 14 GB library was not duplicating models; 93% of its bytes were per-district meshes. Changes: art-library models are always instanced (small scans had been merged into every district: about 2.6 GB); terrain and sea are indexed (about 6x fewer vertices; Godot's position compression was rejected because it broke exact district seams); sea rows merge into quads; flat sea bed is not subdivided; detailed solids collide as boxes (arches, doors, stairs, landings, bridges and fences stay exact); FinalizeFacet --prune-models removes unreferenced library models. Live-edit positions stay exact (no GPU readback, exact undo).
- Samples (district plus referenced models): open sea 470 to 145 KB; coast 4.5 to 4.1 MB; Britain 5.0 to 4.0 MB; Covetous mountains 6.2 to about 3.8 MB.
- Evidence: ArtFidelityTests and SurfacePolishTests updated for merged/indexed meshes; RegionalSeamTests 20/20; DevEditTests 79/0; scripts/verify.ps1 passed.

## 31 October 2026 - Splitting stacks

- Dropping a stack (into the backpack, a container, a trade or onto the world) asks how many to move, as in the original client: Viewer/Network/AmountPrompt.gd offers the whole stack with a slider, Half/All, Enter to move and Escape to cancel; holding Shift moves the whole stack without asking. Part of a stack can be dropped back into its own container to split it there. GameSession.move_item lifts the chosen amount (0x07) and keeps it for ground retries.
- Evidence: StackSplitTests 10/0 (in verify): partial lift amount, split in the same container, whole stack never moved onto itself, single items never split, prompt default, typed amount and limit.
- Gap: not yet exercised against the live server (the owner was signed in on the development account).

## 31 October 2026 - Taller mountains (docs/MOUNTAINS.md)

- Converter relief (Converter/Relief.cs) lifts terrain corners deep inside impassable dry land (mountain rock, cave walls, the dungeon void) by 1.2x their height above the nearest walkable corner, eased in over 7 tiles, up to 32 m; walkable corners never move. TerrainSurface, the collision heightmap and the regional horizon use it (Viewer/Presentation/Relief.gd); relief.u8 is part of the bake fingerprint and scripts/bake-facet.ps1 recomputes it.
- Trammel: 1,757,705 corners lifted. Preview bakes of six Covetous districts show jagged peaks well above the forest instead of mounds; a 3-tile ramp rose like a curtain wall and was rejected.
- Evidence: ReliefTests 6/0 (in verify); scripts/verify.ps1 passed.
- Gap: the published Trammel cache shows it only after the next facet bake (with the compression steps and the entrance blockers).

## 31 October 2026 - Open-microphone voice chat and live translation (docs/VOICE_AND_TRANSLATION.md)

- Tap V or click the microphone: the microphone stays open and every sentence is recognized (local Whisper) and said; tap again to close. Holding V is still push-to-talk. A sentence ends after 1.8 s of quiet (owner: 0.9 s cut sentences short), adjustable in the menu (1.2-3.5 s).
- Game menu > Voice and translation > Show everything in: the journal and overhead speech appear in the chosen language, per character and per player. Offline Argos Translate (MIT; CTranslate2 models, langid detection) via scripts/translate-server.py on 127.0.0.1, started on demand by Viewer/Network/Translator.gd; scripts/setup-translation.ps1 installs it (English <-> it, es, fr, de installed here; other languages download when chosen). Presentation only: game logic still reads the server's text.
- Evidence: TranslatorTests 10/0 (in verify); end-to-end through Translator.gd: English and Spanish lines into Italian in 62-860 ms (35-80 ms once warm), service start about 33 s; scripts/verify.ps1 passed.
- Gaps: gumps, item names and the interface are not translated yet; the open microphone was not exercised live with a microphone in this pass.

## 31 October 2026 - Dungeons, cave entrances and death (docs/DUNGEONS.md)

- Underground (Viewer/Presentation/Underground.gd): near UO's void (0x244) or cave land the client goes underground over 1.5 s: no sky or sun, dim cool ambient, black fog closing at 46 m and a warm carried light. Districts with void/cave land draw a cave terrain (CaveGround.gdshader) whose walls fade to black above the head. Runtime only; the baked cache is unchanged.
- Cave entrances: each of the 29 surface entrances (blank blocker tiles 0x3B60-0x3B83) gets an arched rock frame and a dark tunnel (CaveMouth.gdshader, depth-tested on the mouth plane so the slope never hides it and a mountain still does). The blockers become invisible on their next bake.

Esc > Developer > Die/Resurrect uses loopback-only [PreviewDie/[PreviewResurrect (ModernUO Kill/Resurrect); installed on the next server restart.

## Login memory and mouse camera correction

Camera look now starts only with an unhandled right-button press, captures the pointer, consumes relative motion, and releases before GUI routing on mouse-up, Escape or window focus loss. Controllers are off until explicitly enabled; their stick deadzone is 0.35. No idle camera recentering is introduced. Login fields retain the latest submitted account/password, seeded from the existing local development account when no saved login exists. Windows CurrentUser DPAPI encrypts the local vault through anonymous pipes; passwords are never passed as process arguments. Remember is enabled initially, with explicit forget/opt-out controls. Non-Windows secure persistence is not implemented and reports failure.

Visual pass: closer default camera, warmer light, softer ground/roof patterns with distance-aware filtering, 4x MSAA, coordinated low-contrast architectural materials using unchanged publisher texture files, smaller in-world instructions and hidden controller settings after entry. This does not resolve remaining architectural proportions, missing gable/hip reconstruction or crop boundaries. Original sources and server remain untouched.

## Britannia Reborn and server environment â€” 25 October 2026

The project is now Britannia Reborn, with canonical public repository https://github.com/rastlyn80/Britannia-Reborn. Original project code is GPL-3.0-or-later; NOTICE.md retains BSD/CC0 and separately fetched dependency distinctions. README and CONTRIBUTING describe the actual early scope, public setup and data exclusions. The full local history was checked for proprietary inputs, real converted worlds, credentials and saves before publication; none matched the restricted paths or secret-assignment checks. Largest history blob is approximately 14 MB of licensed CC0 art. This is a bounded scan, not a complete transitive dependency/legal audit.

Native global/personal light and weather packets now feed the improved sky, cloud cover, fog, sunlight and sheltered rain/snow presentation. A read-only loopback preview extension supplies the actual upstream regional clock and current weather snapshots every ten seconds. Server core, source maps, gameplay and world state were not edited. A saved graceful restart reloaded 3 mobiles/50 items; the development character then received real clock/light/clear-weather data. Rain and snow screenshots use controlled state fixtures. Details and limits: SERVER_ENVIRONMENT.md.

The Godot title/login panel show Britannia Reborn. Its custom user-directory setting intentionally retains the former UO World Studio vault path; the existing encrypted login remains available. Setup now accepts a custom UO installation path and reuses the configured data directory on subsequent server launches.

Server-owned doors/containers/signs and broader world population remain the next integration milestone. No population or map expansion is claimed by this environment change.

Validation at publication: scripts/verify.ps1 passes 218 offline checks and the actual-crop load; 13 environment checks are included. Live environment and login tests pass after the rename. Controlled day/night contrast, cloud motion and real ceiling shelter pass on both Forward+ and OpenGL (3 GPU checks each). Moving-camera floor/doorway stability adds four passing checks; return-view RGB differences remain below 0.0017. No proprietary capture is included in Git.

### Armed Lizardman trial

Verified the classic spear/mace body split (35/36), exact rigid Third Dawn source meshes, atlas and right-hand attachment in ten Forward+ frames. See [source and reproduction](RATMEN_AND_LIZARDMEN.md#armed-lizardman-fitting-trial).
